Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The magnetometry operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 607. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 342 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the magnetometry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 607 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 342. QED.
